    31	 * The multiplication in this routine will exceed UINT32_MAX
    32	 * and wrap around for large values of u. This is expected and required.
    33	 */
    34	#ifdef CBMC
    35	#pragma CPROVER check push
    36	#pragma CPROVER check disable "unsigned-overflow"

    38	
    39	/**
    40	 * Compute round(u * 2 / MLKEM_Q).
    41	 *
    42	 * @spec{Compress_1 from @[FIPS203, Eq (4.7)].}
    43	 *
    44	 * @reference{Part of poly_tomsg() in the reference implementation @[REF].}
    45	 *
    46	 * @param u Unsigned canonical modulus modulo MLKEM_Q to be compressed.
    47	 *
    48	 * @return Compressed value.
    49	 */
    50	static MLK_INLINE uint8_t mlk_scalar_compress_d1(int16_t u)
    51	__contract__(
    52	  requires(0 <= u && u <= MLKEM_Q - 1)
    53	  ensures(return_value < 2)
    54	  ensures(return_value == (((uint32_t)u * 2 + MLKEM_Q / 2) / MLKEM_Q) % 2)  )
    55	{
    56	  /* Compute as follows:
    57	   * ```
    58	   * round(u * 2 / MLKEM_Q)
    59	   *   = round(u * 2 * (2^31 / MLKEM_Q) / 2^31)
    60	   *  ~= round(u * 2 * round(2^31 / MLKEM_Q) / 2^31)
    61	   * ```
    62	   */
    63	  /* check-magic: 1290168 == 2*round(2^31 / MLKEM_Q) */
    64	  uint32_t d0 = (uint32_t)u * 1290168;
    65	  /* Unsigned shifting by 31 positions leaves only the top bit. */
    66	  return (uint8_t)((d0 + ((uint32_t)1u << 30)) >> 31);
    67	}
    68	#ifdef CBMC
    69	#pragma CPROVER check pop
    70	#endif
    71	
    72	/*
    73	 * The multiplication in this routine will exceed UINT32_MAX
    74	 * and wrap around for large values of u. This is expected and required.
    75	 */
    76	#ifdef CBMC
    77	#pragma CPROVER check push
    78	#pragma CPROVER check disable "unsigned-overflow"
    79	#endif
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Compute round(u * 16 / MLKEM_Q) % 16.
    83	 *
    84	 * @spec{Compress_4 from @[FIPS203, Eq (4.7)].}
    85	 *
    86	 * @reference{Embedded into `poly_compress()` in the reference
    87	 * implementation @[REF].}
    88	 *
    89	 * @param u Unsigned canonical modulus modulo MLKEM_Q to be compressed.
    90	 *
    91	 * @return Compressed value.
    92	 */
    93	static MLK_INLINE uint8_t mlk_scalar_compress_d4(int16_t u)
    94	__contract__(
    95	  requires(0 <= u && u <= MLKEM_Q - 1)
    96	  ensures(return_value < 16)
    97	  ensures(return_value == (((uint32_t)u * 16 + MLKEM_Q / 2) / MLKEM_Q) % 16))
    98	{
    99	  /* Compute as follows:
   100	   * ```
   101	   * round(u * 16 / MLKEM_Q)
   102	   *   = round(u * 16 * (2^28 / MLKEM_Q) / 2^28)
   103	   *  ~= round(u * 16 * round(2^28 / MLKEM_Q) / 2^28)
   104	   * ```
   105	   */
   106	  /* check-magic: 1290160 == 16 * round(2^28 / MLKEM_Q) */
   107	  uint32_t d0 = (uint32_t)u * 1290160;
   108	  /* The return value is < 16, so not altered by the conversion to uint8_t. */
   109	  return (uint8_t)((d0 + ((uint32_t)1u << 27)) >> 28); /* round(d0/2^28) */
   110	}
   111	#ifdef CBMC
   112	#pragma CPROVER check pop
   113	#endif
   114	
   115	/**
   116	 * Compute round(u * MLKEM_Q / 16).
   117	 *
   118	 * @spec{Decompress_4 from @[FIPS203, Eq (4.8)].}
   119	 *
   120	 * @reference{Embedded into `poly_decompress()` in the reference

FILE=/home/girish/THESIS-2026/mlkem-native_af4c5abd/mlkem/src/compress.c
PATTERN=mlk_scalar_compress_d1[[:space:]]*\(
FILE_PRESENT=YES
PATTERN_FOUND=YES
FIRST_MATCH_LINE=733
   698	    {
   699	      /* mlk_ct_sel_int16(MLKEM_Q_HALF, 0, b) is `Decompress_1(b != 0)`
   700	       * as per @[FIPS203, Eq (4.8)]. */
   701	
   702	      /* Prevent the compiler from recognizing this as a bit selection */
   703	      uint8_t mask = mlk_value_barrier_u8((uint8_t)(1u << j));
   704	      r->coeffs[8 * i + j] = mlk_ct_sel_int16(MLKEM_Q_HALF, 0, msg[i] & mask);
   705	    }
   706	  }
   707	  mlk_assert_abs_bound(r, MLKEM_N, MLKEM_Q);
   708	}
   709	
   710	/* Reference: `poly_tomsg()` in the reference implementation @[REF].
   711	 *            - In contrast to the reference implementation, we assume
   712	 *              unsigned canonical coefficients here.
   713	 *              The reference implementation works with coefficients
   714	 *              in the range [-(MLKEM_Q-1), MLKEM_Q-1].
   715	 */
   716	MLK_INTERNAL_API
   717	void mlk_poly_tomsg(uint8_t msg[MLKEM_INDCPA_MSGBYTES], const mlk_poly *r)
   718	{
   719	  unsigned i;
   720	  mlk_assert_bound(r, MLKEM_N, 0, MLKEM_Q);
   721	
   722	  for (i = 0; i < MLKEM_N / 8; i++)
   723	  __loop__(invariant(i <= MLKEM_N / 8)
   724	           decreases(MLKEM_N / 8 - i))
   725	  {
   726	    unsigned j;
   727	    msg[i] = 0;
   728	    for (j = 0; j < 8; j++)
   729	    __loop__(
   730	      invariant(i <= MLKEM_N / 8 && j <= 8)
   731	      decreases(8 - j))
   732	    {
   733	      uint32_t t = mlk_scalar_compress_d1(r->coeffs[8 * i + j]);
   734	      msg[i] |= (uint8_t)(t << j);
   735	    }
   736	  }
   737	}

   595	/* Reference: `indcpa_dec()` in the reference implementation @[REF].
   596	 *            - We use a mulcache for the scalar product.
   597	 *            - We include buffer zeroization. */
   598	MLK_INTERNAL_API
   599	int mlk_indcpa_dec(uint8_t m[MLKEM_INDCPA_MSGBYTES],
   600	                   const uint8_t c[MLKEM_INDCPA_BYTES],
   601	                   const uint8_t sk[MLKEM_INDCPA_SECRETKEYBYTES],
   602	                   MLK_CONFIG_CONTEXT_PARAMETER_TYPE context)
   603	{
   604	  int ret = 0;
   605	  MLK_ALLOC(b, mlk_polyvec, 1, context);
   606	  MLK_ALLOC(skpv, mlk_polyvec, 1, context);
   607	  MLK_ALLOC(v, mlk_poly, 1, context);
   608	  MLK_ALLOC(sb, mlk_poly, 1, context);
   609	  MLK_ALLOC(b_cache, mlk_polyvec_mulcache, 1, context);
   610	
   611	  if (b == NULL || skpv == NULL || v == NULL || sb == NULL || b_cache == NULL)
   612	  {
   613	    ret = MLK_ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY;
   614	    goto cleanup;
   615	  }
   616	
   617	  mlk_unpack_ciphertext(b, v, c);
   618	  mlk_unpack_sk(skpv, sk);
   619	
   620	  mlk_polyvec_ntt(b);
   621	  mlk_polyvec_mulcache_compute(b_cache, b);
   622	  mlk_polyvec_basemul_acc_montgomery_cached(sb, skpv, b, b_cache);
   623	  mlk_poly_invntt_tomont(sb);
   624	
   625	  mlk_poly_sub(v, sb);
   626	  mlk_poly_reduce(v);
   627	
   628	  mlk_poly_tomsg(m, v);
   629	
   630	cleanup:
   631	  /* Specification: Partially implements
   632	   * @[FIPS203, Section 3.3, Destruction of intermediate values] */
   633	  MLK_FREE(b_cache, mlk_polyvec_mulcache, 1, context);
   634	  MLK_FREE(sb, mlk_poly, 1, context);
   635	  MLK_FREE(v, mlk_poly, 1, context);
   636	  MLK_FREE(skpv, mlk_polyvec, 1, context);
   637	  MLK_FREE(b, mlk_polyvec, 1, context);
   638	  return ret;
   639	}

    17	#
    18	# Makefile.common implements what we consider to be some best
    19	# practices for using cbmc for software verification.
    20	#
    21	# Section I gives default values for a large number of Makefile
    22	# variables that control
    23	#   * how your code is built (include paths, etc),
    24	#   * what program transformations are applied to your code (loop
    25	#     unwinding, etc), and
    26	#   * what properties cbmc checks for in your code (memory safety, etc).
    27	#
    28	# These variables are defined below with definitions of the form
    29	#   VARIABLE ?= DEFAULT_VALUE
    30	# meaning VARIABLE is set to DEFAULT_VALUE if VARIABLE has not already
    31	# been given a value.
    32	#
    33	# For your project, you can override these default values with

    53	#   * Create a subdirectory <DIR>.

    56	#   * Write a makefile with the name <DIR>/Makefile that looks

   102	##
   103	## Remember that Makefile.common and Makefile-project-defines are
   104	## included into the proof Makefile in your proof directory, so all
   105	## relative pathnames defined there should be relative to your proof
   106	## directory.
   107	
   108	################################################################
   109	# Define the layout of the source tree and the proof subtree
   110	#
   111	# Generally speaking,
   112	#
   113	#   SRCDIR = the root of the repository
   114	#   CBMC_ROOT = /srcdir/cbmc
   115	#   PROOF_ROOT = /srcdir/cbmc/proofs
   116	#   PROOF_SOURCE = /srcdir/cbmc/sources
   117	#   PROOF_INCLUDE = /srcdir/cbmc/include
   118	#   PROOF_STUB = /srcdir/cbmc/stubs
   119	#   PROOFDIR = the directory containing the Makefile for your proof
   120	#
   121	# The path /srcdir/cbmc used in the example above is determined by the
   122	# setup script cbmc-starter-kit-setup.  Projects usually create a cbmc
   123	# directory somewhere in the source tree, and run the setup script in
   124	# that directory.  The value of CBMC_ROOT becomes the absolute path to
   125	# that directory.
   126	#
   127	# The location of that cbmc directory in the source tree affects the
   128	# definition of SRCDIR, which is defined in terms of the relative path
   129	# from a proof directory to the repository root.  The definition is
   130	# usually determined by the setup script cbmc-starter-kit-setup and
   131	# written to Makefile-template-defines, but you can override it for a
   132	# project in Makefile-project-defines and for a specific proof in the
   133	# Makefile for the proof.
   134	
   135	# Absolute path to the directory containing this Makefile.common
   136	# See https://ftp.gnu.org/old-gnu/Manuals/make-3.80/html_node/make_17.html
   137	#
   138	# Note: We compute the absolute paths to the makefiles in MAKEFILE_LIST
   139	# before we filter the list of makefiles for %/Makefile.common.
   140	# Otherwise an invocation of the form "make -f Makefile.common" will set
   141	# MAKEFILE_LIST to "Makefile.common" which will fail to match the
   142	# pattern %/Makefile.common.
   143	#
   144	MAKEFILE_PATHS = $(foreach makefile,$(MAKEFILE_LIST),$(abspath $(makefile)))
   145	PROOF_ROOT = $(dir $(filter %/Makefile.common,$(MAKEFILE_PATHS)))
   146	
   147	CBMC_ROOT = $(shell dirname $(PROOF_ROOT))
   148	PROOF_SOURCE = $(CBMC_ROOT)/sources
   149	PROOF_INCLUDE = $(CBMC_ROOT)/include
   150	PROOF_STUB = $(CBMC_ROOT)/stubs
   151	
   152	# Flag to pass to goto-cc to make all static symbols globally visible. Leave it
   153	# unset or set it to --export-file-local-symbols to enable this behavior. Else,
   154	# selectively enable access to such symbols via each proof's Makefile.
   155	EXPORT_FILE_LOCAL_SYMBOLS =
   156	
   157	SRCDIR ?= $(abspath $(PROOF_ROOT)/../../)
   158	LITANI ?= litani
   159	PROJECT_NAME = "mlkem-native"
   160	
   161	# SRCDIR is the path to the root of the source tree
   162	# This is a default definition that is frequently overridden in
   163	# another Makefile, see the discussion of SRCDIR above.
   164	SRCDIR ?= $(abspath ../..)
   165	
   166	# PROOFDIR is the path to the directory containing the proof harness
   167	PROOFDIR ?= $(abspath .)
   168	
   169	################################################################
   170	# Define how to run CBMC
   171	
   172	# Do property checking with the external SAT solver given by
   173	# EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER.  Do coverage checking with the default solver,
   174	# since coverage checking requires the use of an incremental solver.
   175	# The EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER variable is typically set (if it is at all)
   176	# as an environment variable or as a makefile variable in
   177	# Makefile-project-defines.
   178	#
   179	# For a particular proof, if the default solver is faster, do property
   180	# checking with the default solver by including this definition in the
   181	# proof Makefile:
   182	#         USE_EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER =
   183	#
   184	ifneq ($(strip $(EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER)),)
   185	   USE_EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER ?= --external-sat-solver $(EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER)
   186	endif
   187	CHECKFLAGS += $(USE_EXTERNAL_SAT_SOLVER)
   188	
   189	# Job pools
   190	# For version of Litani that are new enough (where `litani print-capabilities`
   191	# prints "pools"), proofs for which `EXPENSIVE = true` is set can be added to a
   192	# "job pool" that restricts how many expensive proofs are run at a time. All
   193	# other proofs will be built in parallel as usual.
   194	#
   195	# In more detail: all compilation, instrumentation, and report jobs are run with
   196	# full parallelism as usual, even for expensive proofs. The CBMC jobs for
   197	# non-expensive proofs are also run in parallel. The only difference is that the
   198	# CBMC safety checks and coverage checks for expensive proofs are run with a
   199	# restricted parallelism level. At any one time, only N of these jobs are run at
   200	# once, amongst all the proofs.
   201	#
   202	# To configure N, Litani needs to be initialized with a pool called "expensive".
   203	# For example, to only run two CBMC safety/coverage jobs at a time from amongst
   204	# all the proofs, you would initialize litani like
   205	#         litani init --pools expensive:2
   206	# The run-cbmc-proofs.py script takes care of this initialization through the
   207	# --expensive-jobs-parallelism flag.
   208	#
   209	# To enable this feature, set
   210	# the ENABLE_POOLS variable when running Make, like
   211	#         `make ENABLE_POOLS=true report`
   212	# The run-cbmc-proofs.py script takes care of this through the
   213	# --restrict-expensive-jobs flag.
   214	
   215	ifeq ($(strip $(ENABLE_POOLS)),)
   216	  POOL =
   217	  INIT_POOLS =
   218	else ifeq ($(strip $(EXPENSIVE)),)
   219	  POOL =
   220	  INIT_POOLS =
   221	else
   222	  POOL = --pool expensive
   223	  INIT_POOLS = --pools expensive:1
   224	endif
   225	
   226	# Similar to the pool feature above. If Litani is new enough, enable
   227	# profiling CBMC's memory use.
   228	ifeq ($(strip $(ENABLE_MEMORY_PROFILING)),)
   229	  MEMORY_PROFILING =
   230	else
   231	  MEMORY_PROFILING = --profile-memory
   232	endif
   233	
   234	# Property checking flags
   235	#
   236	# Each variable below controls a specific property checking flag
   237	# within CBMC. If desired, a property flag can be disabled within
   238	# a particular proof by nulling the corresponding variable when CBMC's default
   239	# is not to perform such checks, or setting to --no-<CHECK>-check when CBMC's
   240	# default is to perform such checks. For instance, the following lines:
   241	#
   242	#     CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_CHECK = --no-pointer-check
   243	#     CBMC_FLAG_UNSIGNED_OVERFLOW_CHECK =
   244	#
   245	# would disable pointer checks and unsigned overflow checks with CBMC flag
   246	# within:
   247	#   * an entire project when added to Makefile-project-defines
   248	#   * a specific proof when added to the harness Makefile
   249	
   250	CBMC_FLAG_MALLOC_MAY_FAIL ?= --malloc-may-fail --malloc-fail-null # alloc may fail with returning NULL
   251	CBMC_FLAG_BOUNDS_CHECK ?= # set to --no-bounds-check to disable
   252	CBMC_FLAG_CONVERSION_CHECK ?= --conversion-check
   253	CBMC_FLAG_DIV_BY_ZERO_CHECK ?= # set to --no-div-by-zero-check to disable
   254	CBMC_FLAG_FLOAT_OVERFLOW_CHECK ?= --float-overflow-check
   255	CBMC_FLAG_NAN_CHECK ?= --nan-check
   256	CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_CHECK ?= #set to --no-pointer-check to disable
   257	CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_OVERFLOW_CHECK ?= --pointer-overflow-check
   258	CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_PRIMITIVE_CHECK ?= # set to --no-pointer-primitive-check to disable
   259	CBMC_FLAG_SIGNED_OVERFLOW_CHECK ?= # set to --no-signed-overflow-check to disable
   260	CBMC_FLAG_UNDEFINED_SHIFT_CHECK ?= # set to --no-undefined-shift-check to disable
   261	CBMC_FLAG_UNSIGNED_OVERFLOW_CHECK ?= --unsigned-overflow-check
   262	CBMC_FLAG_UNWINDING_ASSERTIONS ?= # set to --no-unwinding-assertions to disable
   263	CBMC_DEFAULT_UNWIND ?= --unwind 1
   264	CBMC_FLAG_FLUSH ?= --flush
   265	
   266	# Determine SMT back-end and prover combination chosen by the user
   267	ifeq ($(findstring --smt2,$(CBMCFLAGS)),--smt2) # User has chosen SMT2 (which defaults to Z3)
   268	CBMCFLAGS := $(subst --smt2,,$(CBMCFLAGS)) # Remove --smt2 from CBMCFLAGS
   269	BACKEND_OPTION := --smt2
   270	PROVER_NAME=Z3
   271	SMT_FORMAT := --z3
   272	else ifeq ($(findstring --bitwuzla,$(CBMCFLAGS)),--bitwuzla) # User has chosen Bitwuzla
   273	CBMCFLAGS := $(subst --bitwuzla,,$(CBMCFLAGS)) # Remove --bitwuzla from CBMCFLAGS
   274	BACKEND_OPTION := --bitwuzla
   275	PROVER_NAME=Bitwuzla
   276	SMT_FORMAT := --bitwuzla
   277	else ifeq ($(findstring --cvc5,$(CBMCFLAGS)),--cvc5) # User has chosen cvc5
   278	CBMCFLAGS := $(subst --cvc5,,$(CBMCFLAGS)) # Remove --cvc5 from CBMCFLAGS
   279	BACKEND_OPTION := --cvc5
   280	PROVER_NAME=cvc5
   281	SMT_FORMAT := --cvc5
   282	endif
   283	
   284	# CBMC 6.0.0 enables all standard checks by default, which can make coverage analysis
   285	# very slow. See https://github.com/diffblue/cbmc/issues/8389
   286	# For now, we disable these checks when generating coverage info.
   287	COVERFLAGS ?= --no-standard-checks --malloc-may-fail --malloc-fail-null
   288	
   289	# CBMC flags used for property checking
   290	
   291	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_BOUNDS_CHECK)
   292	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_CONVERSION_CHECK)
   293	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_DIV_BY_ZERO_CHECK)
   294	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_FLOAT_OVERFLOW_CHECK)
   295	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_NAN_CHECK)
   296	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_CHECK)
   297	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_OVERFLOW_CHECK)
   298	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_POINTER_PRIMITIVE_CHECK)
   299	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_SIGNED_OVERFLOW_CHECK)
   300	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_UNDEFINED_SHIFT_CHECK)
   301	CHECKFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_UNSIGNED_OVERFLOW_CHECK)
   302	
   303	# Additional CBMC flag to CBMC control verbosity.
   304	#
   305	# Meaningful values are
   306	# 0 none
   307	# 1 only errors
   308	# 2 + warnings
   309	# 4 + results
   310	# 6 + status/phase information
   311	# 8 + statistical information
   312	# 9 + progress information
   313	# 10 + debug info
   314	#
   315	# Uncomment the following line or set in Makefile-project-defines
   316	# CBMC_VERBOSITY ?= --verbosity 4
   317	
   318	# Additional CBMC flag to control how CBMC treats static variables.
   319	#
   320	# NONDET_STATIC is a list of flags of the form --nondet-static
   321	# and --nondet-static-exclude VAR.  The --nondet-static flag causes
   322	# CBMC to initialize static variables with unconstrained value
   323	# (ignoring initializers and default zero-initialization).  The
   324	# --nondet-static-exclude VAR excludes VAR for the variables
   325	# initialized with unconstrained values.
   326	NONDET_STATIC ?=
   327	
   328	# Flags to pass to goto-cc for compilation and linking
   329	#
   330	# Note that we always use gcc for pre-processing with goto-cc
   331	# in order to get consistent header files on all platforms,
   332	# rather than using $(CC) (which might be preferred for compilation
   333	# on some platforms such as macOS/Darwin)
   334	COMPILE_FLAGS ?= -Wall -Werror --native-compiler gcc
   335	LINK_FLAGS ?= -Wall -Werror
   336	EXPORT_FILE_LOCAL_SYMBOLS ?= --export-file-local-symbols
   337	
   338	# During instrumentation, it adds models of C library functions
   339	ADD_LIBRARY_FLAG := --add-library
   340	
   341	# Preprocessor include paths -I...
   342	INCLUDES ?=
   343	INCLUDES += -I$(PROOFDIR)
   344	INCLUDES += -I$(SRCDIR)/proofs/cbmc
   345	INCLUDES += -I$(SRCDIR)/mlkem
   346	INCLUDES += -I$(SRCDIR)/mlkem/src/
   347	INCLUDES += -I$(SRCDIR)/mlkem/src/fips202
   348	
   349	# Preprocessor definitions -D...
   350	DEFINES ?=
   351	
   352	# CBMC object model
   353	#
   354	# CBMC_OBJECT_BITS is the number of bits in a pointer CBMC uses for
   355	# the id of the object to which a pointer is pointing.  CBMC uses 8
   356	# bits for the object id by default. The remaining bits in the pointer
   357	# are used for offset into the object.  This limits the size of the
   358	# objects that CBMC can model.  This Makefile defines this bound on
   359	# object size to be CBMC_MAX_OBJECT_SIZE.  You are likely to get
   360	# unexpected results if you try to malloc an object larger than this
   361	# bound.
   362	CBMC_OBJECT_BITS ?= 8
   363	
   364	# CBMC loop unwinding (Normally set in the proof Makefile)
   365	#
   366	# UNWINDSET is a list of pairs of the form foo.1:4 meaning that
   367	# CBMC should unwind loop 1 in function foo no more than 4 times.
   368	# For historical reasons, the number 4 is one more than the number
   369	# of times CBMC actually unwinds the loop.
   370	UNWINDSET ?=
   371	
   372	# CBMC early loop unwinding (Normally set in the proof Makefile)
   373	#
   374	# Most users can ignore this variable.
   375	#
   376	# This variable exists to support the use of loop and function
   377	# contracts, two features under development for CBMC.  Checking the
   378	# assigns clause for function contracts and loop invariants currently
   379	# assumes loop-free bodies for loops and functions with contracts
   380	# (possibly after replacing nested loops with their own loop
   381	# contracts).  To satisfy this requirement, it may be necessary to
   382	# unwind some loops before the function contract and loop invariant
   383	# transformations are applied to the goto program.  This variable
   384	# CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET is identical to UNWINDSET, and we assume that the
   385	# loops mentioned in CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET and UNWINDSET are disjoint.
   386	CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET ?=
   387	
   388	# CBMC function removal (Normally set set in the proof Makefile)
   389	#
   390	# REMOVE_FUNCTION_BODY is a list of function names.  CBMC will "undefine"
   391	# the function, and CBMC will treat the function as having no side effects
   392	# and returning an unconstrained value of the appropriate return type.
   393	# The list should include the names of functions being stubbed out.
   394	REMOVE_FUNCTION_BODY ?=
   395	
   396	# CBMC function pointer restriction (Normally set in the proof Makefile)
   397	#
   398	# RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER is a list of function pointer restriction
   399	# instructions of the form:
   400	#
   401	#    <fun_id>.function_pointer_call.<N>/<fun_id>[,<fun_id>]*
   402	#
   403	# The function pointer call number <N> in the specified function gets
   404	# rewritten to a case switch over a finite list of functions.
   405	# If some possible target functions are omitted from the list a counter
   406	# example trace will be found by CBMC, i.e. the transformation is sound.
   407	# If the target functions are file-local symbols, then mangled names must
   408	# be used.
   409	RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER ?=
   410	
   411	# The project source files (Normally set set in the proof Makefile)
   412	#
   413	# PROJECT_SOURCES is the list of project source files to compile,
   414	# including the source file defining the function under test.
   415	PROJECT_SOURCES ?=
   416	
   417	# The proof source files (Normally set in the proof Makefile)
   418	#
   419	# PROOF_SOURCES is the list of proof source files to compile, including
   420	# the proof harness, and including any function stubs being used.
   421	PROOF_SOURCES ?=
   422	
   423	# The number of seconds that CBMC should be allowed to run for before
   424	# being forcefully terminated. Currently, this is set to be less than
   425	# the time limit for a CodeBuild job, which is eight hours. If a proof
   426	# run takes longer than the time limit of the CI environment, the
   427	# environment will halt the proof run without updating the Litani
   428	# report, making the proof run appear to "hang".
   429	CBMC_TIMEOUT ?= 21600
   430	
   431	# CBMC string abstraction
   432	#
   433	# Replace all uses of char * by a struct that carries that string,
   434	# and also the underlying allocation and the C string length.
   435	STRING_ABSTRACTION ?=
   436	ifdef STRING_ABSTRACTION
   437	  ifneq ($(strip $(STRING_ABSTRACTION)),)
   438	    CBMC_STRING_ABSTRACTION := --string-abstraction
   439	  endif
   440	endif
   441	
   442	# Optional configuration library flags
   443	OPT_CONFIG_LIBRARY ?=
   444	CBMC_OPT_CONFIG_LIBRARY := $(CBMC_FLAG_MALLOC_MAY_FAIL) $(CBMC_STRING_ABSTRACTION)
   445	
   446	# Proof writers could add function contracts in their source code.
   447	# These contracts are ignored by default, but may be enabled in two distinct
   448	# contexts using the following two variables:
   449	# 1. To check whether one or more function contracts are sound with respect to
   450	#    the function implementation, CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS should be a list of
   451	#    function names. Use CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS_REC to check contracts on
   452	#    recursive functions.
   453	# 2. To replace calls to certain functions with their correspondent function
   454	#    contracts, USE_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS should be a list of function names.
   455	#    One must check separately whether a function contract is sound before
   456	#    replacing it in calling contexts.
   457	CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS ?=
   458	CBMC_CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS := $(patsubst %,--enforce-contract %, $(CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS))
   459	
   460	CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS_REC ?=
   461	CBMC_CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS_REC := $(patsubst %,--enforce-contract-rec %, $(CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS_REC))
   462	
   463	USE_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS ?=
   464	CBMC_USE_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS := $(patsubst %,--replace-call-with-contract %, $(USE_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS))
   465	
   466	CODE_CONTRACTS := $(CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS)$(USE_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS)$(APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS)
   467	
   468	# Proof writers may also apply function contracts using the Dynamic Frame
   469	# Condition Checking (DFCC) mode. For more information on DFCC,
   470	# please see https://diffblue.github.io/cbmc/contracts-dev-spec-dfcc.html.
   471	USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES ?=
   472	ifdef USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES
   473	  ifneq ($(strip $(USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES)),)
   474	    CBMC_USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES := $(CBMC_OPT_CONFIG_LIBRARY) --dfcc $(HARNESS_ENTRY) $(CBMC_CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS_REC)
   475	  endif
   476	endif
   477	
   478	# Similarly, proof writers could also add loop contracts in their source code
   479	# to obtain unbounded correctness proofs. Unlike function contracts, loop
   480	# contracts are not reusable and thus are checked and used simultaneously.
   481	# These contracts are also ignored by default, but may be enabled by setting
   482	# the APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS variable.
   483	APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS ?=
   484	ifdef APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS
   485	  ifneq ($(strip $(APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS)),)
   486	    CBMC_APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS := --apply-loop-contracts
   487	  endif
   488	endif
   489	
   490	# The default unwind should only be used in DFCC mode without loop contracts.
   491	# When loop contracts are applied, we only unwind specified loops.
   492	# If any loops remain after loop contracts have been applied, CBMC might try
   493	# to unwind the program indefinitely, because we do not pass default unwind
   494	# (i.e., --unwind 1) to CBMC when in DFCC mode.
   495	# We must not use a default unwind command in DFCC mode, because contract instrumentation
   496	# introduces loops encoding write set inclusion checks that must be dynamically unwound during
   497	# symex.
   498	ifneq ($(strip $(USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES)),)
   499	ifneq ($(strip $(APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS)),)
   500	  UNWIND_0500_FLAGS=$(CBMC_UNWINDSET) $(CBMC_CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET) $(CBMC_FLAG_UNWINDING_ASSERTIONS)
   501	  UNWIND_0500_DESC="$(PROOF_UID): unwinding specified subset of loops"
   502	else
   503	  UNWIND_0500_FLAGS=$(CBMC_UNWINDSET) $(CBMC_CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET) $(CBMC_DEFAULT_UNWIND) $(CBMC_FLAG_UNWINDING_ASSERTIONS)
   504	  UNWIND_0500_DESC="$(PROOF_UID): unwinding all loops"
   505	endif
   506	endif
   507	
   508	# Silence makefile output (eg, long litani commands) unless VERBOSE is set.
   509	ifndef VERBOSE
   510	    MAKEFLAGS := $(MAKEFLAGS) -s
   511	endif
   512	
   513	################################################################
   514	################################################################
   515	## Section II: This section defines the process of running a proof
   516	##
   517	## There should be no reason to edit anything below this line.
   518	
   519	################################################################
   520	# Paths
   521	
   522	CBMC ?= cbmc
   523	GOTO_ANALYZER ?= goto-analyzer
   524	GOTO_CC ?= goto-cc
   525	GOTO_INSTRUMENT ?= goto-instrument
   526	CRANGLER ?= crangler
   527	VIEWER ?= cbmc-viewer
   528	VIEWER2 ?= cbmc-viewer
   529	CMAKE ?= cmake
   530	
   531	GOTODIR ?= $(PROOFDIR)/gotos
   532	LOGDIR ?= $(PROOFDIR)/logs
   533	
   534	PROJECT ?= project
   535	PROOF ?= proof
   536	
   537	HARNESS_GOTO ?= $(GOTODIR)/$(HARNESS_FILE)
   538	PROJECT_GOTO ?= $(GOTODIR)/$(PROJECT)
   539	PROOF_GOTO ?= $(GOTODIR)/$(PROOF)
   540	
   541	################################################################
   542	# Useful macros for values that are hard to reference
   543	SPACE :=$() $()
   544	COMMA :=,
   545	
   546	################################################################
   547	# CBMC team recommend that --no-array-field-sensitivity should never be used
   548	# with CBMC 6.9.0 and above when --slice-formula has been specified
   549	ifeq ($(findstring --no-array-field-sensitivity,$(CBMCFLAGS)),--no-array-field-sensitivity)
   550	$(error CBMC option --no-array-field-sensitivity is believed to be detrimental to performance in CBMC v6.9.0 and has been removed from the CBMC Makefiles -- do you really want this?)
   551	endif
   552	
   553	################################################################
   554	# CBMC team recommend that --slice-formula should _always_ be used
   555	# so we reject it here if it is already specified in a per-function Makefile
   556	ifeq ($(findstring --slice-formula,$(CBMCFLAGS)),--slice-formula)
   557	$(error CBMC option --slice-formula is set by default in Makefile.common, so should not be specified in a function-specific Makefile)
   558	endif
   559	
   560	################################################################
   561	# CBMC flags common to all proofs
   562	CBMCFLAGS += $(CBMC_FLAG_FLUSH)
   563	CBMCFLAGS += --object-bits $(CBMC_OBJECT_BITS)
   564	CBMCFLAGS += --slice-formula
   565	
   566	################################################################
   567	# Set C compiler defines
   568	DEFINES += -DCBMC=1
   569	DEFINES += -DCBMC_OBJECT_BITS=$(CBMC_OBJECT_BITS)
   570	DEFINES += -DCBMC_MAX_OBJECT_SIZE="(SIZE_MAX>>(CBMC_OBJECT_BITS+1))"
   571	
   572	
   573	ifndef MLKEM_K
   574	    $(error MLKEM_K not set)
   575	endif
   576	
   577	ifeq ($(MLKEM_K),2)
   578	MLK_CONFIG_PARAMETER_SET=512
   579	else ifeq ($(MLKEM_K),3)
   580	MLK_CONFIG_PARAMETER_SET=768
   581	else ifeq ($(MLKEM_K),4)
   582	MLK_CONFIG_PARAMETER_SET=1024
   583	else
   584	$(error Invalid value for MLKEM_K)
   585	endif
   586	
   587	DEFINES += -DMLK_CONFIG_FILE="\"mlkem_native_config_cbmc.h\""
   588	DEFINES += -DMLK_CONFIG_PARAMETER_SET=$(MLK_CONFIG_PARAMETER_SET)
   589	# Give visibility to all static functions
   590	DEFINES += -Dstatic=
   591	DEFINES += -DMLK_INLINE=
   592	DEFINES += -DMLK_ALWAYS_INLINE=
   593	
   594	# CI currently assumes cbmc invocation has at most one --unwindset
   595	
   596	# UNWINDSET is designed for user code (i.e., proof and project code)
   597	ifdef UNWINDSET
   598	  ifneq ($(strip $(UNWINDSET)),)
   599	    CBMC_UNWINDSET := --unwindset $(subst $(SPACE),$(COMMA),$(strip $(UNWINDSET)))
   600	  endif
   601	endif
   602	
   603	# CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET is designed for CPROVER library functions
   604	ifdef CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET
   605	  ifneq ($(strip $(CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET)),)
   606	    CBMC_CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET := --unwindset $(subst $(SPACE),$(COMMA),$(strip $(CPROVER_LIBRARY_UNWINDSET)))
   607	  endif
   608	endif
   609	
   610	CBMC_REMOVE_FUNCTION_BODY := $(patsubst %,--remove-function-body %, $(REMOVE_FUNCTION_BODY))
   611	
   612	ifdef RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER
   613	  ifneq ($(strip $(RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER)),)
   614	    CBMC_RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER := $(patsubst %,--restrict-function-pointer %, $(RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER))
   615	  endif
   616	endif
   617	
   618	################################################################
   619	# Targets for rewriting source files with crangler
   620	
   621	# Construct crangler configuration files
   622	#
   623	# REWRITTEN_SOURCES is a list of crangler output files source.i.
   624	# This target assumes that for each source.i
   625	#   * source.i_SOURCE is the path to a source file,
   626	#   * source.i_FUNCTIONS is a list of functions (may be empty)
   627	#   * source.i_OBJECTS is a list of variables (may be empty)
   628	# This target constructs the crangler configuration file source.i.json
   629	# of the form
   630	#   {
   631	#     "sources":   [ "/proj/code.c" ],
   632	#     "includes":  [ "/proj/include" ],
   633	#     "defines":   [ "VAR=1" ],
   634	#     "functions": [ {"function_name": ["remove static"]} ],
   635	#     "objects":   [ {"variable_name": ["remove static"]} ],
   636	#     "output":    "source.i"
   637	#   }
   638	# to remove the static attribute from function_name and variable_name
   639	# in the source file source.c and write the result to source.i.
   640	#
   641	# This target assumes that filenames include no spaces and that
   642	# the INCLUDES and DEFINES variables include no spaces after -I
   643	# and -D.  For example, use "-DVAR=1" and not "-D VAR=1".
   644	#
   645	# Define *_SOURCE, *_FUNCTIONS, and *_OBJECTS in the proof Makefile.
   646	# The string source.i is usually an absolute path $(PROOFDIR)/code.i
   647	# to a file in the proof directory that contains the proof Makefile.
   648	# The proof Makefile usually includes the definitions
   649	#   $(PROOFDIR)/code.i_SOURCE = /proj/code.c
   650	#   $(PROOFDIR)/code.i_FUNCTIONS = function_name
   651	#   $(PROOFDIR)/code.i_OBJECTS = variable_name
   652	# Because these definitions refer to PROOFDIR that is defined in this
   653	# Makefile.common, these definitions must appear after the inclusion
   654	# of Makefile.common in the proof Makefile.
   655	#
   656	$(foreach rs,$(REWRITTEN_SOURCES),$(eval $(rs).json: $($(rs)_SOURCE)))
   657	$(foreach rs,$(REWRITTEN_SOURCES),$(rs).json):
   658		echo '{'\
   659		  '"sources": ['\
   660		  '"$($(@:.json=)_SOURCE)"'\
   661		  '],'\
   662		  '"includes": ['\
   663		    '$(subst $(SPACE),$(COMMA),$(patsubst -I%,"%",$(strip $(INCLUDES))))' \
   664		  '],'\
   665		  '"defines": ['\
   666		    '$(subst $(SPACE),$(COMMA),$(patsubst -D%,"%",$(subst ",\",$(strip $(DEFINES)))))' \
   667		  '],'\
   668		  '"functions": ['\
   669		    '{'\
   670		      '$(subst ~, ,$(subst $(SPACE),$(COMMA),$(patsubst %,"%":["remove~static"],$($(@:.json=)_FUNCTIONS))))' \
   671		    '}'\
   672		  '],'\
   673		  '"objects": ['\
   674		    '{'\
   675		      '$(subst ~, ,$(subst $(SPACE),$(COMMA),$(patsubst %,"%":["remove~static"],$($(@:.json=)_OBJECTS))))' \
   676		    '}'\
   677		  '],'\
   678		  '"output": "$(@:.json=)"'\
   679		'}' > $@
   680	
   681	# Rewrite source files with crangler
   682	#
   683	$(foreach rs,$(REWRITTEN_SOURCES),$(eval $(rs): $(rs).json))
   684	$(REWRITTEN_SOURCES):
   685		$(LITANI) add-job \
   686		  --command \
   687		  '$(CRANGLER) $@.json' \
   688		  --inputs $($@_SOURCE) \
   689		  --outputs $@ \
   690		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/crangler-$(subst /,_,$(subst .,_,$@))-log.txt \
   691		  --interleave-stdout-stderr \
   692		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   693		  --ci-stage build \
   694		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): removing static"
   695	
   696	################################################################
   697	# Build targets that make the relevant .goto files
   698	
   699	# Compile project sources
   700	$(PROJECT_GOTO)0100.goto: $(PROJECT_SOURCES) $(REWRITTEN_SOURCES)
   701		$(LITANI) add-job \
   702		  --command \
   703		    '$(GOTO_CC) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(COMPILE_FLAGS) $(EXPORT_FILE_LOCAL_SYMBOLS) $(INCLUDES) $(DEFINES) $^ -o $@' \
   704		  --inputs $^ \
   705		  --outputs $@ \
   706		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/project_sources-log.txt \
   707		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   708		  --ci-stage build \
   709		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): building project binary"
   710	
   711	# Compile proof sources
   712	$(PROOF_GOTO)0100.goto: $(PROOF_SOURCES)
   713		$(LITANI) add-job \
   714		  --command \
   715		    '$(GOTO_CC) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(COMPILE_FLAGS) $(EXPORT_FILE_LOCAL_SYMBOLS) $(INCLUDES) $(DEFINES) $^ -o $@' \
   716		  --inputs $^ \
   717		  --outputs $@ \
   718		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/proof_sources-log.txt \
   719		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   720		  --ci-stage build \
   721		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): building proof binary"
   722	
   723	# Remove function bodies from project sources
   724	$(PROJECT_GOTO)0200.goto: $(PROJECT_GOTO)0100.goto
   725		$(LITANI) add-job \
   726		  --command \
   727		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(CBMC_REMOVE_FUNCTION_BODY) $^ $@' \
   728		  --inputs $^ \
   729		  --outputs $@ \
   730		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/remove_function_body-log.txt \
   731		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   732		  --ci-stage build \
   733		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): removing function bodies from project sources"
   734	
   735	# Link project and proof sources into the proof harness
   736	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0100.goto: $(PROOF_GOTO)0100.goto $(PROJECT_GOTO)0200.goto
   737		$(LITANI) add-job \
   738		  --command '$(GOTO_CC) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) --function $(HARNESS_ENTRY) $^ $(LINK_FLAGS) -o $@' \
   739		  --inputs $^ \
   740		  --outputs $@ \
   741		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/link_proof_project-log.txt \
   742		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   743		  --ci-stage build \
   744		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): linking project to proof"
   745	
   746	# Restrict function pointers
   747	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0200.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0100.goto
   748		$(LITANI) add-job \
   749		  --command \
   750		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(CBMC_RESTRICT_FUNCTION_POINTER) --remove-function-pointers $^ $@' \
   751		  --inputs $^ \
   752		  --outputs $@ \
   753		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/restrict_function_pointer-log.txt \
   754		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   755		  --ci-stage build \
   756		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): restricting function pointers in project sources"
   757	
   758	# Fill static variable with unconstrained values
   759	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0300.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0200.goto
   760	ifneq ($(strip $(CODE_CONTRACTS)),)
   761		$(LITANI) add-job \
   762		  --command 'cp $^ $@' \
   763		  --inputs $^ \
   764		  --outputs $@ \
   765		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/nondet_static-log.txt \
   766		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   767		  --ci-stage build \
   768		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): not setting static variables to nondet (will do during contract instrumentation)"
   769	else
   770		$(LITANI) add-job \
   771		  --command \
   772		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(NONDET_STATIC) $^ $@' \
   773		  --inputs $^ \
   774		  --outputs $@ \
   775		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/nondet_static-log.txt \
   776		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   777		  --ci-stage build \
   778		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): setting static variables to nondet"
   779	endif
   780	
   781	# Link CPROVER library if DFCC mode is on
   782	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0400.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0300.goto
   783	ifneq ($(strip $(USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES)),)
   784		$(LITANI) add-job \
   785		  --command \
   786		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(ADD_LIBRARY_FLAG) $(CBMC_OPT_CONFIG_LIBRARY) $^ $@' \
   787		  --inputs $^ \
   788		  --outputs $@ \
   789		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/linking-library-models-log.txt \
   790		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   791		  --ci-stage build \
   792		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): linking CPROVER library"
   793	else
   794		$(LITANI) add-job \
   795		  --command 'cp $^ $@' \
   796		  --inputs $^ \
   797		  --outputs $@ \
   798		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/linking-library-models-log.txt \
   799		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   800		  --ci-stage build \
   801		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): not linking CPROVER library"
   802	endif
   803	
   804	# Early unwind all loops on DFCC mode; otherwise, only unwind loops in proof and project code
   805	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0500.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0400.goto
   806	ifneq ($(strip $(USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES)),)
   807		$(LITANI) add-job \
   808		  --command \
   809		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(UNWIND_0500_FLAGS) $^ $@' \
   810		  --inputs $^ \
   811		  --outputs $@ \
   812		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/unwind_loops-log.txt \
   813		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   814		  --ci-stage build \
   815		  --description $(UNWIND_0500_DESC)
   816	else ifneq ($(strip $(CODE_CONTRACTS)),)
   817		$(LITANI) add-job \
   818		  --command \
   819			'$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(CBMC_UNWINDSET) $(CBMC_FLAG_UNWINDING_ASSERTIONS) $^ $@' \
   820		  --inputs $^ \
   821		  --outputs $@ \
   822		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/unwind_loops-log.txt \
   823		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   824		  --ci-stage build \
   825		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): unwinding loops in proof and project code"
   826	else
   827		$(LITANI) add-job \
   828		  --command 'cp $^ $@' \
   829		  --inputs $^ \
   830		  --outputs $@ \
   831		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/unwind_loops-log.txt \
   832		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   833		  --ci-stage build \
   834		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): not unwinding loops"
   835	endif
   836	
   837	# Replace function contracts, check function contracts, instrument for loop contracts
   838	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0600.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0500.goto
   839		$(LITANI) add-job \
   840		  --command \
   841		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_USE_DYNAMIC_FRAMES) $(NONDET_STATIC) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) $(CBMC_CHECK_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS) $(CBMC_USE_FUNCTION_CONTRACTS) $(CBMC_APPLY_LOOP_CONTRACTS) $^ $@' \
   842		  --inputs $^ \
   843		  --outputs $@ \
   844		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/check_function_contracts-log.txt \
   845		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   846		  --ci-stage build \
   847		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): checking function contracts"
   848	
   849	# Omit initialization of unused global variables (reduces problem size)
   850	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0700.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0600.goto
   851		$(LITANI) add-job \
   852		  --command \
   853		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) --slice-global-inits $^ $@' \
   854		  --inputs $^ \
   855		  --outputs $@ \
   856		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/slice_global_inits-log.txt \

   858		  --ci-stage build \
   859		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): slicing global initializations"
   860	
   861	# Omit unused functions (sharpens coverage calculations)
   862	$(HARNESS_GOTO)0800.goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0700.goto
   863		$(LITANI) add-job \
   864		  --command \
   865		    '$(GOTO_INSTRUMENT) $(CBMC_VERBOSITY) --drop-unused-functions $^ $@' \
   866		  --inputs $^ \
   867		  --outputs $@ \
   868		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/drop_unused_functions-log.txt \
   869		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   870		  --ci-stage build \
   871		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): dropping unused functions"
   872	
   873	# Final name for proof harness
   874	$(HARNESS_GOTO).goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO)0800.goto
   875		$(LITANI) add-job \
   876		  --command 'cp $< $@' \
   877		  --inputs $^ \
   878		  --outputs $@ \
   879		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
   880		  --ci-stage build \
   881		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): copying final goto-binary"

   891	  endif

  1042		    --config $(PROOFDIR)/cbmc-viewer.json" \
  1043		  --inputs $^ \
  1044		  --outputs $(PROOFDIR)/report \
  1045		  --pipeline-name "$(PROOF_UID)" \
  1046		  --stdout-file $(LOGDIR)/viewer-log.txt \
  1047		  --ci-stage report \
  1048		  --description "$(PROOF_UID): generating report"
  1049	
  1050	litani-path:
  1051		@echo $(LITANI)
  1052	
  1053	# ##############################################################
  1054	# Phony Rules
  1055	#
  1056	#	These rules provide a convenient way to run a single proof up to a
  1057	#	certain stage. Users can browse into a proof directory and run
  1058	#	"make -Bj 3 report" to generate a report for just that proof, or
  1059	#	"make goto" to build the goto binary. Under the hood, this runs litani
  1060	#	for just that proof.
  1061	
  1062	_goto: $(HARNESS_GOTO).goto
  1063	goto:
  1064		@ echo Running 'litani init'
  1065		$(LITANI) init $(INIT_POOLS) --project $(PROJECT_NAME)
  1066		@ echo Running 'litani add-job'
  1067		$(MAKE) -B _goto
  1068		@ echo Running 'litani build'
  1069		$(LITANI) run-build
  1070
